## 6. 后端接口设计

### 6.1 接口总览

**统一前缀：**

| 类型 | 前缀 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| 对外接口 | `/api/v1/m22/refractive` | 供前端页面调用，需登录态与功能权限 |
| 内部接口 | `/internal/v1/m22/refractive` | 供其他模块调用，需服务间鉴权，不走用户登录态 |

**接口清单：**

| 编号 | 方法 | 路径 | 说明 | 权限 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| | | **P01 屈光经营看板** | | |
| API-01 | GET | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/dashboard` | 取看板八卡 + 漏斗速览 + 术式结构 + 医师 TOP5 | `m22:06:view` |
| API-02 | GET | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/dashboard/cards` | 只取八张指标卡（轻量刷新） | `m22:06:view` |
| API-03 | GET | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/dashboard/status` | 取看板取数状态与缺失来源清单 | `m22:06:view` |
| | | **P02 咨询转化漏斗** | | |
| API-04 | GET | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/funnel` | 取七环节漏斗与转化率 | `m22:06:view` |
| API-05 | GET | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/funnel/groups` | 取漏斗分组对比（渠道/术式/医师） | `m22:06:view` |
| API-06 | POST | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/funnel/export` | 生成漏斗快照并落报表实例（只读，不改业务数据） | `m22:06:export` |
| | | **P03 术式结构与价格带** | | |
| API-07 | GET | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/procedures` | 取术式结构与价格带矩阵 | `m22:06:view` |
| API-08 | GET | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/procedures/{refractive_type}` | 取单术式明细与相邻期对照 | `m22:06:view` |
| API-09 | GET | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/procedures/structure-change` | 取结构变化指数 SCI 与逐术式变化 | `m22:06:view` |
| | | **P04 医师业绩分析** | | |
| API-10 | GET | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/doctors` | 取医师业绩列表与排名 | `m22:06:view` |
| API-11 | GET | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/doctors/{doctor_id}/trend` | 取单医师逐月趋势与术式结构 | `m22:06:view` |
| API-12 | POST | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/doctors/compare` | 多医师横向对比（只读，不改业务数据） | `m22:06:view` |
| | | **P05 渠道与投放 ROI · 台账** | | |
| API-13 | POST | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/ad-spends` | 新建投放台账（支持 Idempotency-Key） | `m22:06:create` |
| API-14 | POST | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/ad-spends/{spend_no}/submit` | 提交台账进入待审批 | `m22:06:create` |
| API-15 | POST | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/ad-spends/{spend_no}/approve` | 审批通过台账 | `m22:06:approve` |
| API-16 | POST | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/ad-spends/{spend_no}/reject` | 驳回台账 | `m22:06:approve` |
| API-17 | POST | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/ad-spends/{spend_no}/void` | 作废已审批台账并通知 M11-08 | `m22:06:edit` |
| API-18 | GET | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/ad-spends` | 台账分页查询 | `m22:06:view` |
| | | **P05 渠道与投放 ROI · 分析** | | |
| API-19 | GET | `/internal/v1/m22/ad-spend` | **内部契约 P-02**：按渠道与期间取投放成本快照 | 内部服务 |
| API-20 | GET | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/channels` | 取渠道经营效果与 ROI 排名 | `m22:06:view` |
| API-21 | GET | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/channels/{channel}/trend` | 取单渠道逐月 ROI 与投放构成 | `m22:06:view` |
| | | **P06 术后质量与经济性** | | |
| API-22 | GET | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/quality` | 取质量四性 + 经济性对照 | `m22:06:view` |
| API-23 | GET | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/quality/indicators/{indicator_code}` | 取单指标逐月趋势与阈值线 | `m22:06:view` |
| API-24 | GET | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/quality/{refractive_type}/detail` | 取单术式的医师下钻与再手术明细 | `m22:06:view` |
| | | **P07 屈光趋势与预测** | | |
| API-25 | GET | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/trends` | 取序列实测点与预测点（分数组返回） | `m22:06:view` |
| API-26 | GET | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/trends/forecast` | 只取预测段与置信区间 | `m22:06:view` |
| API-27 | GET | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/trends/anomalies` | 取异常点清单与触发规则 | `m22:06:view` |
| API-28 | POST | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/trends/recalculate` | 重算预测并覆盖同主键旧值（支持 Idempotency-Key） | `m22:06:exec` |
| | | **P08 屈光报表导出** | | |
| API-29 | POST | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/exports` | 生成导出任务（支持 Idempotency-Key） | `m22:06:export` |
| API-30 | GET | `/api/v1/m22/refractive/exports/{export_no}` | 取导出任务状态与下载链接 | `m22:06:export` |

**接口统计：**

| 页面 | 接口数 | 编号范围 | 其中内部契约 |
|---|---|---|---|
| P01 屈光经营看板 | 3 | API-01 ~ API-03 | 0 |
| P02 咨询转化漏斗 | 3 | API-04 ~ API-06 | 0 |
| P03 术式结构与价格带 | 3 | API-07 ~ API-09 | 0 |
| P04 医师业绩分析 | 3 | API-10 ~ API-12 | 0 |
| P05 渠道与投放 ROI | 9 | API-13 ~ API-21 | 1（API-19） |
| P06 术后质量与经济性 | 3 | API-22 ~ API-24 | 0 |
| P07 屈光趋势与预测 | 4 | API-25 ~ API-28 | 0 |
| P08 屈光报表导出 | 2 | API-29 ~ API-30 | 0 |
| **合计** | **30** | **API-01 ~ API-30** | **1** |

**内部契约登记：**

| 契约编号 | 方法 | 路径 | 实现接口 | 调用方 | SLA |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| P-02 | GET | `/internal/v1/m22/ad-spend` | API-19 | M11-08 | P95 ≤ 300ms |

**路径设计说明：**

1. 本子模块所有对外接口统一挂在 `/api/v1/m22/refractive` 下，按页面分组路径段（`dashboard`/`funnel`/`procedures`/`doctors`/`ad-spends`/`channels`/`quality`/`trends`/`exports`），前端可据此按需懒加载。
2. 内部契约 P-02 的路径 `/internal/v1/m22/ad-spend` 由共享规范 §8 一次性定义，**M11-08 侧已写死该路径**，本模块不得改动任何一个字符。注意该路径不带 `refractive` 段，与对外接口前缀不同，这是契约总线的统一约定，不是笔误。
3. 统计查询一律用 `GET`；只有「生成快照 / 重算预测 / 生成导出 / 台账写操作」用 `POST`。`POST` 类统计接口在文档中均标注「只读，不改业务数据」。
4. 所有 `POST` 写接口均支持 `Idempotency-Key` 请求头，幂等记录写入 Redis `m22:idem:{biz}:{key}`，TTL 24 小时。

---

### 6.2 统一响应格式与错误码

**成功响应：**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {},
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**失败响应：**

```json
{
  "code": "2206002",
  "message": "统计期间格式不正确，应为 YYYY-MM",
  "data": null,
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务错误码清单：**

| 错误码 | HTTP 状态 | 错误信息 | 触发场景 | 前端处理建议 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `"2206001"` | 500 | 屈光分析内部错误 | 未预期异常 | 提示稍后重试，记录 trace_id |
| `"2206002"` | 400 | 统计期间格式不正确 | `period` 非 `YYYY-MM`，或起止倒置 | 定位到期间控件并标红 |
| `"2206003"` | 400 | 科室不存在或不开展屈光业务 | `dept_id` 非法 | 提示后重置为全院 |
| `"2206004"` | 400 | 查询跨度超过上限 | 期间跨度超过 24 个月（漏斗）或 60 期（趋势） | 提示并自动收缩区间 |
| `"2206005"` | 400 | 分组方式与渠道过滤冲突 | 非 `CHANNEL` 分组却传了 `channel` | 清空渠道过滤 |
| `"2206006"` | 409 | 漏斗数据倒挂 | 上游环节人数小于下游环节人数 | 标红并提示联系数据管理员 |
| `"2206007"` | 409 | 价格带归属冲突 | 实收净额落在无归属边界 | 提示检查价目表映射 |
| `"2206008"` | 409 | 收入数据非快照来源 | 试图用推算值代替 M16 快照 | 拒绝并提示请联系运维 |
| `"2206009"` | 409 | 指标分母口径不一致 | 同一页面内同类比率分母不同 | 提示口径冲突 |
| `"2206010"` | 400 | 对比对象数量超限 | 医师对比超过 5 人 | 限制勾选数量 |
| `"2206011"` | 403 | 日志表不可修改 | 调用日志更新或删除 | 不应出现，记安全告警 |
| `"2206012"` | 404 | 渠道不存在 | `channel` 不在九值内 | 重置为全部渠道 |
| `"2206013"` | 409 | 台账批次重复 | 同 `(channel, period, batch_no)` 已存在 | 定位到既有台账 |
| `"2206014"` | 400 | 投放金额必须大于 0 | 新建台账金额 ≤ 0 | 标红金额输入框 |
| `"2206015"` | 400 | 渠道取值非法 | 渠道不在九值内 | 下拉重置 |
| `"2206016"` | 409 | 台账状态不允许该操作 | 状态机不允许的迁移 | 刷新列表后重试 |
| `"2206017"` | 400 | 作废必须填写原因 | 作废未填 `void_reason` | 弹出原因输入框 |
| `"2206018"` | 409 | 审批人与提交人不能相同 | 自审自批 | 提示转交他人审批 |
| `"2206019"` | 409 | ROI 分母为 0 | 投放成本为 0 却计算 ROI | 返回 null，显示「—」 |
| `"2206020"` | 409 | 零金额台账不得计入投放成本 | `spend_amount = 0` 却标记 `is_p2_included` | 拒绝并记日志 |
| `"2206021"` | 409 | 质量指标必须引用 M11-07 | 试图在 M22 内重算四性 | 拒绝并提示走 P-14 |
| `"2206022"` | 409 | 相关系数样本期数不足 | `corr_sample_periods < 6` | 返回 null，显示「—」 |
| `"2206023"` | 409 | 告警级别与上游不一致 | M22 自判阈值导致级别不同 | 以 M11-07 为准并记日志 |
| `"2206024"` | 409 | 质量与经济性期间不一致 | 两张表期间不匹配 | 提示重新查询 |
| `"2206025"` | 400 | 预测行缺少必需字段 | FORECAST 缺方法或置信区间 | 拒绝写入 |
| `"2206026"` | 400 | 实测行不应带预测方法 | ACTUAL 带 `forecast_method` | 拒绝写入 |
| `"2206027"` | 400 | 置信区间未覆盖预测值 | `ci_lower > value` 或 `value > ci_upper` | 拒绝写入 |
| `"2206028"` | 400 | 基准期不能晚于预测期 | `forecast_base_period >= period` | 调整基准期 |
| `"2206029"` | 400 | 历史数据不足无法预测 | 基准期数不足 3 期 | 提示延长查询区间 |
| `"2206030"` | 400 | 预测期数超过上限 | `forecast_horizon > 12` | 限制输入 |
| `"2206031"` | 400 | 手工预测必须填写依据 | `MANUAL` 方法未填备注 | 弹出依据输入框 |
| `"2206032"` | 409 | 预测覆盖未留痕 | 重算未记录前后值 | 记 `"2206032"` 并告警 |
| `"2206033"` | 409 | 数据不完整暂不预测 | 上游 `source_status` 非 `COMPLETE` | 提示稍后重试 |
| `"2206034"` | 404 | 报表模板不存在或未启用 | `template_code` 非法或非 `ACTIVE` | 重置模板下拉 |
| `"2206035"` | 403 | 导出未写审计日志 | 审计写入失败 | 拒绝导出并告警 |
| `"2206036"` | 400 | 导出行数超出上限 | 超过 50,000 行 | 提示缩小范围 |
| `"2206037"` | 410 | 下载链接已过期 | 超过 7 天 | 提示重新生成 |
| `"229001"` | 500 | M22 模块通用错误 | 跨子模块共用异常 | 提示稍后重试 |

> **错误码号段说明**：本子模块使用 `"2206001"` ~ `"2206037"` 共 37 个业务码，加模块通用码 `"229001"`。号段 `"2206038"` ~ `"2206999"` 为本子模块预留扩展区。

---

### 6.3 接口详细设计

#### API-01 取屈光经营看板

**请求**

```text
GET /api/v1/m22/refractive/dashboard?period=2026-10&dept_id=0&compare_type=MOM&include_forecast=false
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "period": "2026-10",
    "dept_id": 0,
    "compare_period": "2026-09",
    "cards": [
      {"metric_code": "RA01001", "metric_name": "咨询量", "metric_value": 1284, "metric_unit": "COUNT",
       "compare_value": 1185, "change_rate": 0.083544, "value_type": "ACTUAL", "source_module": "M11"},
      {"metric_code": "RA03001", "metric_name": "手术量", "metric_value": 231, "metric_unit": "COUNT",
       "compare_value": 218, "change_rate": 0.059633, "value_type": "ACTUAL", "source_module": "M11"},
      {"metric_code": "REF_REVENUE_SNAP", "metric_name": "屈光收入", "metric_value": 4014830.00,
       "metric_unit": "AMOUNT", "compare_value": 3611670.00, "change_rate": 0.111632,
       "value_type": "ACTUAL", "source_module": "M16"},
      {"metric_code": "REF_AVG_TICKET_SNAP", "metric_name": "客单价", "metric_value": 15932.66,
       "metric_unit": "AMOUNT", "compare_value": 15185.20, "change_rate": 0.049223,
       "value_type": "ACTUAL", "source_module": "M16"},
      {"metric_code": "RA02007", "metric_name": "总转化率", "metric_value": 0.179906,
       "metric_unit": "RATE", "compare_value": 0.191032, "change_rate": -0.058235,
       "value_type": "ACTUAL", "source_module": "M11"},
      {"metric_code": "REF_ROI_CHANNEL", "metric_name": "渠道 ROI", "metric_value": 21.527617,
       "metric_unit": "RATIO", "compare_value": null, "change_rate": null,
       "value_type": "ACTUAL", "source_module": "M22"},
      {"metric_code": "EI_UCVA", "metric_name": "有效性指数", "metric_value": 1.050000,
       "metric_unit": "RATIO", "compare_value": 1.030000, "change_rate": 0.019417,
       "value_type": "ACTUAL", "source_module": "M11"},
      {"metric_code": "COMPLICATION_RATE", "metric_name": "并发症率", "metric_value": 0.012000,
       "metric_unit": "RATE", "compare_value": 0.012000, "change_rate": 0.000000,
       "value_type": "ACTUAL", "source_module": "M11"}
    ],
    "funnel_preview": {
      "consult_count": 1284, "arrive_count": 836, "exam_count": 672, "plan_count": 368,
      "book_count": 286, "surface_count": 252, "done_count": 231,
      "overall_cvr": 0.179906, "lost_stage": "PLAN", "lost_count": 304
    },
    "procedure_top": [
      {"refractive_type": "SMILE", "case_count": 126, "case_share": 0.545455},
      {"refractive_type": "FS_LASIK", "case_count": 62, "case_share": 0.268398},
      {"refractive_type": "ICL", "case_count": 28, "case_share": 0.121212},
      {"refractive_type": "TRANSPRK", "case_count": 15, "case_share": 0.064935},
      {"refractive_type": "SMART", "case_count": 2, "case_share": 0.008660}
    ],
    "doctor_top": [
      {"doctor_id": 10231, "doctor_name": "张医师", "surgery_case_count": 48, "quality_score": 92.40},
      {"doctor_id": 10232, "doctor_name": "李医师", "surgery_case_count": 41, "quality_score": 90.10}
    ],
    "source_status": "COMPLETE",
    "missing_sources": [],
    "collect_batch_no": "CB20261031-0001",
    "generated_at": "2026-10-31 18:20:31"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验期间格式与科室归属（`"2206002"`/`"2206003"`）。2) 并发调 M11-08 P-15 取咨询量、手术量、总转化率；调 M11-07 P-14 取有效性指数与并发症率；调 M16-01 `POST /internal/v1/billing/statistics` 取收入与客单价快照。3) 调本模块渠道 ROI 汇总（内部方法，非接口）得到 `REF_ROI_CHANNEL`。4) 合并各来源结果，任一失败则该卡片值置 null 且 `source_status` 降级为 `PARTIAL`，记录缺失来源。5) 按 `compare_type` 填充 `compare_value` 与 `change_rate`，比较基期无数据时 `change_rate` 为 null 而非 0。
**错误码：** `"2206002"`、`"2206003"`、`"2206008"`、`"2206009"`、`"229001"`

#### API-02 只取看板指标卡

**请求**

```text
GET /api/v1/m22/refractive/dashboard/cards?period=2026-10&dept_id=0
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "period": "2026-10",
    "dept_id": 0,
    "cards": [
      {"metric_code": "RA01001", "metric_value": 1284, "metric_unit": "COUNT"},
      {"metric_code": "RA03001", "metric_value": 231, "metric_unit": "COUNT"},
      {"metric_code": "REF_REVENUE_SNAP", "metric_value": 4014830.00, "metric_unit": "AMOUNT"},
      {"metric_code": "REF_AVG_TICKET_SNAP", "metric_value": 15932.66, "metric_unit": "AMOUNT"},
      {"metric_code": "RA02007", "metric_value": 0.179906, "metric_unit": "RATE"},
      {"metric_code": "REF_ROI_CHANNEL", "metric_value": 21.527617, "metric_unit": "RATIO"},
      {"metric_code": "EI_UCVA", "metric_value": 1.050000, "metric_unit": "RATIO"},
      {"metric_code": "COMPLICATION_RATE", "metric_value": 0.012000, "metric_unit": "RATE"}
    ],
    "cached": true,
    "cache_ttl_seconds": 268,
    "source_status": "COMPLETE"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 先查 Redis `m22:refractive:dashboard:cards:{period}:{dept_id}`，命中直接返回并置 `cached = true`。2) 未命中则走与 API-01 相同的取数流程，但只取八张卡不做术式与医师明细。3) 写回缓存，TTL 300 秒；期间未冻结（当月）时 TTL 缩短为 60 秒。4) 不回写任何数据库表。
**错误码：** `"2206002"`、`"2206003"`、`"229001"`

#### API-03 取看板取数状态

**请求**

```text
GET /api/v1/m22/refractive/dashboard/status?period=2026-10&dept_id=0
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "period": "2026-10",
    "source_status": "PARTIAL",
    "source_status_reason": "M17-04 成本接口超时，再手术成本不可用",
    "missing_sources": [
      {"module": "M17", "api": "GET /internal/v1/m17/dept/profit",
       "impact_metric": ["REF_GROSS_MARGIN_SNAP"], "impact_page": ["P06"],
       "reason": "TIMEOUT", "retry_count": 2, "last_attempt_at": "2026-10-31 18:20:30"}
    ],
    "degraded_count": 1,
    "collect_batch_no": "CB20261031-0001",
    "checked_at": "2026-10-31 18:20:31"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 读取本期采集批次记录（M22-02 `m22_collect_batch`）与各来源健康状态。2) 汇总 `source_status`：任一来源失败为 `FAILED`，任一降级为 `PARTIAL`，全部成功且未超时效为 `COMPLETE`。3) 超时效（`is_stale`）时置 `STALE` 并给出数据截至时间。4) 只读 M22-02 表，不发起新的上游调用。
**错误码：** `"2206002"`、`"2206003"`、`"229001"`

#### API-04 取七环节漏斗

**请求**

```text
GET /api/v1/m22/refractive/funnel?period_from=2026-09&period_to=2026-10&dept_id=0&group_by=ALL
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "period_from": "2026-09",
    "period_to": "2026-10",
    "dept_id": 0,
    "group_by": "ALL",
    "stages": [
      {"stage_code": "CONSULT", "stage_name": "咨询量", "count": 1284, "numerator": 1284,
       "denominator": null, "cvr": null, "cvr_source": "FROM_M11"},
      {"stage_code": "ARRIVE", "stage_name": "到院量", "count": 836, "numerator": 836,
       "denominator": 1284, "cvr": 0.651090, "cvr_source": "FROM_M11"},
      {"stage_code": "EXAM", "stage_name": "术前检查", "count": 672, "numerator": 672,
       "denominator": 836, "cvr": 0.803828, "cvr_source": "FROM_M11"},
      {"stage_code": "PLAN", "stage_name": "出方案", "count": 368, "numerator": 368,
       "denominator": 672, "cvr": 0.547619, "cvr_source": "FROM_M11"},
      {"stage_code": "BOOK", "stage_name": "预约", "count": 286, "numerator": 286,
       "denominator": 368, "cvr": 0.777174, "cvr_source": "FROM_M11"},
      {"stage_code": "SURFACE", "stage_name": "手术", "count": 252, "numerator": 252,
       "denominator": 286, "cvr": 0.881119, "cvr_source": "FROM_M11"},
      {"stage_code": "DONE", "stage_name": "完成", "count": 231, "numerator": 231,
       "denominator": 252, "cvr": 0.916667, "cvr_source": "FROM_M11"}
    ],
    "overall_cvr": 0.179906,
    "lost_stage": "PLAN",
    "lost_count": 304,
    "benchmark": {"stage_code": "PLAN", "benchmark_cvr": 0.55, "health_lower": 0.45, "health_upper": 0.70},
    "source_status": "COMPLETE",
    "missing_sources": []
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验期间跨度不超过 24 个月（`"2206004"`）与非 `CHANNEL` 分组不得带 `channel`（`"2206005"`）。2) 调 M11-08 P-13 `GET /internal/v1/m11/statistics/funnel` 取七环节人数与六环节转化率，**本模块不重算转化率**。3) 校验单调性，出现倒挂返回 `"2206006"` 并整表标 `PARTIAL`。4) 计算 `lost_stage` 与 `lost_count`：按逐环节流失绝对人数取最大者，流失人数 = 上环节人数 − 本环节人数。5) `overall_cvr` 取 M11-08 的 `RA02007`。
**错误码：** `"2206002"`、`"2206004"`、`"2206005"`、`"2206006"`、`"229001"`

#### API-05 取漏斗分组对比

**请求**

```text
GET /api/v1/m22/refractive/funnel/groups?period_from=2026-09&period_to=2026-10&dept_id=0&group_by=CHANNEL
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "group_by": "CHANNEL",
    "groups": [
      {"group_value": "DOUYIN", "group_name": "抖音", "consult_count": 412, "arrive_count": 268,
       "exam_count": 214, "plan_count": 118, "book_count": 92, "surface_count": 81,
       "done_count": 75, "overall_cvr": 0.182039, "lost_stage": "PLAN", "source_status": "COMPLETE"},
      {"group_value": "WECHAT", "group_name": "微信", "consult_count": 286, "arrive_count": 198,
       "exam_count": 162, "plan_count": 92, "book_count": 74, "surface_count": 66,
       "done_count": 61, "overall_cvr": 0.213287, "lost_stage": "PLAN", "source_status": "COMPLETE"},
      {"group_value": "MEITUAN", "group_name": "美团", "consult_count": 231, "arrive_count": 142,
       "exam_count": 108, "plan_count": 52, "book_count": 38, "surface_count": 32,
       "done_count": 29, "overall_cvr": 0.125541, "lost_stage": "PLAN", "source_status": "COMPLETE"}
    ],
    "total_groups": 9,
    "source_status": "COMPLETE"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `group_by` 取值（`"2206005"`）。2) 调 M11-08 P-13 并传 `group_by` 参数取分组结果。3) 每个分组独立计算 `overall_cvr` 与 `lost_stage`，分组内不得跨组汇总。4) 分组值必须与上游枚举一字不差（九渠道 / 十术式 / 医师主键）。5) 某分组人数为 0 时该组不返回，不返回空行。
**错误码：** `"2206002"`、`"2206004"`、`"2206005"`、`"2206012"`、`"229001"`

#### API-06 生成漏斗快照

**请求**

```json
{
  "period_from": "2026-09",
  "period_to": "2026-10",
  "dept_id": 0,
  "group_by": "CHANNEL",
  "template_code": "RF_FUNNEL",
  "export_format": "PDF",
  "include_forecast": false
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "report_no": "RI20261031-0007",
    "export_no": "EX20261031-0003",
    "template_code": "RF_FUNNEL",
    "period": "2026-10",
    "scope_type": "HOSPITAL",
    "status": "GENERATING",
    "source_status": "COMPLETE",
    "created_at": "2026-10-31 18:21:05"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验模板存在且 `ACTIVE`（`"2206034"`）。2) 调 M22-03 的报表实例生成接口建 `m22_report_instance`（`report_category = 'REFRACTIVE'`、`owner_submodule = 'M22_06'`），本模块不建这张表。3) 取漏斗数据写入 `m22_report_row`，逐行带 `source_status` 与 `data_batch_no`。4) 生成导出任务，异步渲染。5) 幂等：同一 `(template_code, period, scope)` 生成中时返回既有 `report_no` 而非新建。**本接口只读，不改任何业务数据。**
**错误码：** `"2206002"`、`"2206004"`、`"2206034"`、`"2206035"`、`"229001"`

#### API-07 取术式结构与价格带

**请求**

```text
GET /api/v1/m22/refractive/procedures?period=2026-10&dept_id=0&aggregate_level=BAND&price_band=ALL
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "period": "2026-10",
    "dept_id": 0,
    "aggregate_level": "BAND",
    "total_case_count": 233,
    "procedure_summary": [
      {"refractive_type": "SMILE", "case_count": 126, "case_share": 0.545455,
       "revenue_snap": 2121840.00, "revenue_share": 0.528503, "avg_ticket_snap": 16840.00},
      {"refractive_type": "FS_LASIK", "case_count": 62, "case_share": 0.268398,
       "revenue_snap": 942400.00, "revenue_share": 0.234730, "avg_ticket_snap": 15200.00},
      {"refractive_type": "ICL", "case_count": 28, "case_share": 0.121212,
       "revenue_snap": 688800.00, "revenue_share": 0.171548, "avg_ticket_snap": 24600.00},
      {"refractive_type": "TRANSPRK", "case_count": 15, "case_share": 0.064935,
       "revenue_snap": 192000.00, "revenue_share": 0.047821, "avg_ticket_snap": 12800.00},
      {"refractive_type": "SMART", "case_count": 2, "case_share": 0.008660,
       "revenue_snap": 23000.00, "revenue_share": 0.005727, "avg_ticket_snap": 11500.00}
    ],
    "bands": [
      {"price_band": "PB2", "band_lower_snap": 10000.00, "band_upper_snap": 15000.00,
       "case_count": 36, "case_share": 0.154506, "revenue_share": 0.098700},
      {"price_band": "PB3", "band_lower_snap": 15000.00, "band_upper_snap": 20000.00,
       "case_count": 85, "case_share": 0.364807, "revenue_share": 0.284200}
    ],
    "high_band_share": 0.381000,
    "cr2": 0.813853,
    "source_status": "COMPLETE",
    "amount_collect_batch_no": "CB20261031-0001"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验期间、科室、聚合层次与价格带取值。2) 调 M11-08 P-15 取各术式例数；调 M16-01 `POST /internal/v1/billing/statistics` 取实收净额快照。3) 按 `[lower, upper)` 左闭右开把实收净额归入价格带，落在边界时归入较高带（`"2206007"` 仅当无法唯一归属）。4) `case_share` 与 `revenue_share` 分别计算并各自校验合计为 1。5) `high_band_share` = (PB5 + PB6) 例数 ÷ 总例数；`cr2` = 手术量前两术式之和 ÷ 总手术量。6) 收入占比必须用 M16 快照，禁止用价目表推算（`"2206008"`）。
**错误码：** `"2206002"`、`"2206003"`、`"2206007"`、`"2206008"`、`"229001"`

**请求**

```text
GET /api/v1/m22/refractive/procedures/SMILE?period=2026-10&dept_id=0
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "refractive_type": "SMILE",
    "period": "2026-10",
    "case_count": 126,
    "case_share": 0.545455,
    "revenue_snap": 2121840.00,
    "avg_ticket_snap": 16840.00,
    "band_distribution": [
      {"price_band": "PB2", "case_count": 8, "case_share": 0.063492},
      {"price_band": "PB3", "case_count": 46, "case_share": 0.365079},
      {"price_band": "PB4", "case_count": 52, "case_share": 0.412698},
      {"price_band": "PB5", "case_count": 16, "case_share": 0.126984},
      {"price_band": "PB6", "case_count": 4, "case_share": 0.031746}
    ],
    "compare_period": "2026-09",
    "compare_case_count": 118,
    "mom_case_rate": 0.067797,
    "yoy_case_rate": 0.215385,
    "doctor_share": [
      {"doctor_id": 10231, "doctor_name": "张医师", "case_count": 41, "share": 0.325397}
    ],
    "source_status": "COMPLETE"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `refractive_type` 属于 M01-07 十术式码之一，否则 `"2206012"`。2) 取本期该术式的例数与收入快照。3) 取本期价格带分布，逐带计算占比。4) 取环比与同比，基期无数据时对应字段为 null。5) 取该术式的医师构成，由多至少排序。
**错误码：** `"2206002"`、`"2206012"`、`"229001"`

#### API-09 取结构变化指数

**请求**

```text
GET /api/v1/m22/refractive/procedures/structure-change?period=2026-10&dept_id=0&compare_period=2026-09
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "period": "2026-10",
    "compare_period": "2026-09",
    "structure_change_index": 0.027800,
    "change_level": "STABLE",
    "details": [
      {"refractive_type": "SMILE", "current_share": 0.545455, "previous_share": 0.534483,
       "delta": 0.010972, "abs_delta": 0.010972},
      {"refractive_type": "FS_LASIK", "current_share": 0.268398, "previous_share": 0.275862,
       "delta": -0.007464, "abs_delta": 0.007464},
      {"refractive_type": "ICL", "current_share": 0.121212, "previous_share": 0.112069,
       "delta": 0.009143, "abs_delta": 0.009143}
    ],
    "source_status": "COMPLETE"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验两期数据均完整，任一 `PARTIAL` 则 `structure_change_index` 返回 null（`"2206006"`）。2) 逐术式计算本期与基期占比，`delta = current − previous`。3) `structure_change_index = 0.5 × Σ abs(delta)`，保留 6 位小数。4) 按指数分级：小于 0.05 为 `STABLE`，0.05~0.15 为 `MODERATE`，大于 0.15 为 `SIGNIFICANT`。5) 十术式必须全部纳入计算，缺失术式按占比 0 处理但需在 `details` 中列出。
**错误码：** `"2206002"`、`"2206006"`、`"229001"`

#### API-10 取医师业绩列表

**请求**

```text
GET /api/v1/m22/refractive/doctors?period=2026-10&dept_id=201&doctor_ids=&sort_by=surgery_case_count&sort_order=desc
```

**响应**

```json

  "data": {

    "items": [
      {"doctor_id": 10231, "doctor_name": "张医师", "surgery_case_count": 48, "case_share": 0.207792,
       "personal_cvr": 0.224100, "complication_rate": 0.000000, "severe_complication_rate": 0.000000,
       "reoperation_rate": 0.020833, "quality_score": 92.40, "volume_rank": 1, "quality_rank": 1,
       "peer_flag": false, "alert_level": null, "data_ref": "ZB202610310100001"},
      {"doctor_id": 10232, "doctor_name": "李医师", "surgery_case_count": 41, "case_share": 0.177489,
       "personal_cvr": 0.190476, "complication_rate": 0.008130, "severe_complication_rate": 0.000000,
       "reoperation_rate": 0.000000, "quality_score": 90.10, "volume_rank": 2, "quality_rank": 2,
       "peer_flag": false, "alert_level": null, "data_ref": "ZB202610310100001"},
      {"doctor_id": 10234, "doctor_name": "陈医师", "surgery_case_count": 33, "case_share": 0.142857,
       "personal_cvr": 0.172800, "complication_rate": 0.023000, "severe_complication_rate": 0.003000,
       "reoperation_rate": 0.030303, "quality_score": 87.20, "volume_rank": 4, "quality_rank": 4,
       "peer_flag": true, "alert_level": "WARN", "data_ref": "ZB202610310100001"}
    ],
    "dept_total": {"surgery_case_count": 231, "complication_rate": 0.012000, "overall_cvr": 0.179906},
    "rank_rule": {"min_sample": 10, "tie_policy": "同名次"},
    "source_status": "COMPLETE"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验期间与科室。2) 调 M11-08 P-15 与 M11-07 P-14 取医师维度指标，**必须同 `calc_batch_no`**，不一致时拒绝四性并与前端提示（`"2206023"`）。3) 调 M16-01 取医师收入快照。4) 计算 `quality_score`（3.4.3 五权重），任一分项缺失时 `quality_score` 为 null 而非用 0 补。5) 手术量小于 10 例的医师 `quality_rank` 为空并置 `peer_flag = true`。6) 名次并列同名次，下一名次跳号。
**错误码：** `"2206002"`、`"2206003"`、`"2206009"`、`"2206023"`、`"229001"`

#### API-12 多医师横向对比

**请求**

```json
{
  "period": "2026-10",
  "dept_id": 201,
  "doctor_ids": [10231, 10232, 10234],
  "metric_codes": ["RA04001", "RA04004", "EI_UCVA", "REF_DOCTOR_QUALITY_SCORE"]
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "period": "2026-10",
    "metric_codes": ["RA04001", "RA04004", "EI_UCVA", "REF_DOCTOR_QUALITY_SCORE"],
    "doctors": [
      {"doctor_id": 10231, "doctor_name": "张医师",
       "values": {"RA04001": 48, "RA04004": 0.000000, "EI_UCVA": 1.080000, "REF_DOCTOR_QUALITY_SCORE": 92.40}},
      {"doctor_id": 10232, "doctor_name": "李医师",
       "values": {"RA04001": 41, "RA04004": 0.008130, "EI_UCVA": 1.050000, "REF_DOCTOR_QUALITY_SCORE": 90.10}},
      {"doctor_id": 10234, "doctor_name": "陈医师",
       "values": {"RA04001": 33, "RA04004": 0.023000, "EI_UCVA": 1.020000, "REF_DOCTOR_QUALITY_SCORE": 87.20}}
    ],

    "source_status": "COMPLETE"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `doctor_ids` 数量不超过 5（`"2206010"`）。2) 校验所有医师属同一科室，跨科室对比不返回。3) 逐个 `metric_code` 从 M22-01 取指标定义（方向、单位），从 M22-02 事实表取本期值。4) 计算科室基准值（该科室同期间均值，不含本人）。5) 任一医师某指标缺失时该格返回 null，不用科室均值填充。**本接口只读，不改业务数据。**
**错误码：** `"2206002"`、`"2206010"`、`"229001"`

#### API-13 新建投放台账

**请求**

```json
{
  "channel": "DOUYIN",
  "period": "2026-10",
  "spend_amount": 26800.00,
  "spend_type": "MEDIA",
  "campaign_code": "DY_SMILE_2026Q4",
  "invoice_no": "FP2026101500123",
  "contract_no": "HT2026-MKT-018",
  "effective_from": "2026-10-01"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "spend_no": "AS20261001-0001",
    "batch_no": "ASB202610-001",
    "channel": "DOUYIN",
    "channel_category": "ONLINE",
    "period": "2026-10",
    "spend_amount": 26800.00,
    "audit_status": "DRAFT",
    "is_p2_included": false,
    "created_at": "2026-10-15 09:12:30"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `Idempotency-Key` 头，命中 Redis `m22:idem:adspend:{key}` 直接返回既有结果。2) 校验渠道九值（`"2206015"`）与金额大于 0（`"2206014"`）。3) 由渠道推导 `channel_category`，并校验与渠道码一致。4) 查同 `(channel, period, batch_no)` 是否已存在（`"2206013"`）。5) 生成 `spend_no` 与 `batch_no`，落库为 `DRAFT`，`is_p2_included` 由触发器置为假。6) 写 `m22_refractive_log`（`log_category = 'AD_SPEND'`、`action_code = 'CREATE'`）。7) 幂等结果 TTL 24 小时。
**错误码：** `"2206013"`、`"2206014"`、`"2206015"`、`"229001"`

#### API-14 提交台账

**请求**

```text
POST /api/v1/m22/refractive/ad-spends/AS20261001-0001/submit
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "spend_no": "AS20261001-0001",
    "audit_status": "PENDING",
    "submitted_by": 20431,
    "submitted_at": "2026-10-15 10:02:11",
    "approval_flow_no": "AP20261015-0031"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 查台账存在且未删除，否则 `"2206013"`。2) 校验状态为 `DRAFT` 或 `REJECTED`，否则 `"2206016"`。3) 状态置 `PENDING`，写 `submitted_by` 与 `submitted_at`。4) 若金额超过 50,000.00 则同时调 M20 `GET /internal/v1/m20/approval/{no}` 建审批流。5) 写审计日志。
**错误码：** `"2206013"`、`"2206016"`、`"229001"`

#### API-15 审批通过台账

**请求**

```json
{
  "spend_no": "AS20261001-0001",
  "approve_remark": "抖音十月信息流投放，合同与发票齐全"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "spend_no": "AS20261001-0001",
    "audit_status": "APPROVED",
    "approved_by": 20099,
    "approved_at": "2026-10-15 14:30:00",
    "is_p2_included": true,
    "affected_period": "2026-10",
    "affected_channel": "DOUYIN"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验状态为 `PENDING`，否则 `"2206016"`。2) 校验审批人 ≠ 提交人，否则 `"2206018"`。3) 状态置 `APPROVED`，写审批人与时间，触发器把 `is_p2_included` 置真。4) 失效 Redis 缓存 `m22:refractive:channel:*:{2026-10}`，使 P05 与 P-02 立即反映新台账。5) 记录 `before_value`/`after_value` 到 `m22_refractive_log`。6) 异步通知 M11-08 数据变更（非阻塞）。
**错误码：** `"2206016"`、`"2206018"`、`"2206020"`、`"229001"`

#### API-16 驳回台账

**请求**

```json
{
  "spend_no": "AS20261015-0003",
  "reject_reason": "线上广告缺少投放合同附件，无法确认真实性"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "spend_no": "AS20261015-0003",
    "audit_status": "REJECTED",
    "reject_reason": "线上广告缺少投放合同附件，无法确认真实性",
    "is_p2_included": false,
    "rejected_at": "2026-10-31 18:25:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验状态为 `PENDING`，否则 `"2206016"`。2) 校验 `reject_reason` 非空（`"2206017"`）。3) 状态置 `REJECTED`，记录原因，`is_p2_included` 保持假。4) 写审计日志，通知提交人。
**错误码：** `"2206016"`、`"2206017"`、`"229001"`

#### API-17 作废台账

**请求**

```json
{
  "spend_no": "AS20261008-0002",
  "void_reason": "KOL 合作取消，费用退回"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "spend_no": "AS20261008-0002",
    "audit_status": "VOIDED",
    "void_reason": "KOL 合作取消，费用退回",
    "is_p2_included": false,
    "previous_spend_snap": 46800.00,
    "current_spend_snap": 26800.00,
    "m11_notify": {"status": "QUEUED", "channel": "DOUYIN", "period": "2026-10"},
    "voided_at": "2026-10-31 18:30:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验状态为 `APPROVED`，否则 `"2206016"`。2) 校验 `void_reason` 非空（`"2206017"`）。3) 状态置 `VOIDED`，`is_p2_included` 由触发器置假。4) 重算该渠道该期间的 `spend_snap` 并返回前后值。5) **必须**把变更推送 M11-08（`AdSpendP2BreachNotice`），推送失败进重试队列最多 5 次，仍失败则记 `WARN` 并告警。6) 写审计日志，记录前后金额。
**错误码：** `"2206016"`、`"2206017"`、`"2206032"`、`"229001"`

#### API-18 台账分页查询

**请求**

```text
GET /api/v1/m22/refractive/ad-spends?period=2026-10&channel=DOUYIN&audit_status=APPROVED&page=1&page_size=20
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "items": [
      {"spend_no": "AS20261001-0001", "channel": "DOUYIN", "period": "2026-10",
       "spend_amount": 26800.00, "spend_type": "MEDIA", "audit_status": "APPROVED",
       "approved_by": 20099, "approved_at": "2026-10-15 14:30:00", "is_p2_included": true},
      {"spend_no": "AS20261008-0002", "channel": "DOUYIN", "period": "2026-10",
       "spend_amount": 20000.00, "spend_type": "KOL", "audit_status": "APPROVED",
       "approved_by": 20099, "approved_at": "2026-10-12 10:15:00", "is_p2_included": true}
    ],
    "total": 2,
    "page": 1,
    "page_size": 20,
    "pages": 1,
    "sum_amount": 46800.00
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 解析分页参数，`page_size` 上限 200。2) 按期间、渠道、状态过滤，默认按 `created_at` 倒序。3) 返回 `pages` 必须等于 `ceil(total / page_size)`，由 Schema `check_pages` 校验。4) 额外汇总当前过滤条件的金额合计，供前端展示。5) 台账金额按 `NUMERIC(18,2)` 返回，不做浮点运算。
**错误码：** `"2206002"`、`"2206012"`、`"229001"`

#### API-19 取投放成本快照（内部契约 P-02）

**请求**

```text
GET /internal/v1/m22/ad-spend?channel=DOUYIN&period=2026-10
GET /internal/v1/m22/ad-spend?channel=DOUYIN&date_from=2026-09-01&date_to=2026-10-31
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "channel": "DOUYIN",
    "spend_snap": 46800.00,
    "period": "2026-10",
    "has_spend_record": true,
    "record_count": 2,
    "audit_filter": "APPROVED",
    "data_status": "COMPLETE",
    "degraded_reason": null,
    "calculated_at": "2026-10-31 18:31:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**无投放记录时的响应（情形三，必须返回 200 而非 404）**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "channel": "REFERRAL",
    "spend_snap": 0.00,
    "period": "2026-10",
    "has_spend_record": false,
    "record_count": 0,
    "audit_filter": "APPROVED",
    "data_status": "COMPLETE",
    "degraded_reason": null,
    "calculated_at": "2026-10-31 18:31:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `channel` 属于九值，否则 `"2206012"`（HTTP 404 仅用于「渠道码不存在」，**不用于「该渠道没投放」**）。2) `period` 与 `date_from`/`date_to` 二选一，都传时以 `period` 为准并记 `WARN`；都不传时默认取上一自然月。3) 按 `(channel, period)` 命中覆盖索引 `idx_m22_refractive_ad_spend_p2`（`WHERE audit_status = 'APPROVED' AND is_deleted = FALSE`），只取 `spend_amount` 求和，**不扫全表**，这是 P95 ≤ 300ms 的关键。4) **只计 `APPROVED`**，`DRAFT`/`PENDING`/`REJECTED`/`VOIDED` 一律不计。5) 有已审批记录时 `has_spend_record = true`（即使合计为 0.00）；无已审批记录时 `spend_snap = 0.00` 且 `has_spend_record = false`。6) 结果缓存 Redis `m22:refractive:adspend:{channel}:{period}`，TTL 600 秒；台账审批/作废时主动失效。7) 服务不可用或超时返回 HTTP 503，此时 M11-08 按契约把 `cost_snap` 置 NULL（**这是与「没投钱」的四情形中的情形四**）。8) 写 `m22_refractive_log`（`log_category = 'SYSTEM'`、`caller_module = 'M11'`、`operator_type = 'INTERNAL_API'`）。
**错误码：** `"2206012"`、`"2206002"`、`"229001"`（服务不可用时返回 HTTP 503）

#### API-20 取渠道经营效果

**请求**

```text
GET /api/v1/m22/refractive/channels?period_from=2026-10&period_to=2026-10&dept_id=0&attribution_model=FIRST_TOUCH
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "period_from": "2026-10",
    "period_to": "2026-10",
    "items": [
      {"channel": "DOUYIN", "channel_category": "ONLINE", "consult_count": 412,
       "arrive_count": 268, "surface_count": 81, "spend_snap": 46800.00, "has_spend_record": true,
       "revenue_snap": 1580420.00, "roi": 33.769658, "roi_source": "M22_CALC",
       "cpa": 202.597403, "arrive_cost": 55.97, "attribution_model": "FIRST_TOUCH",
       "attribution_window_days": 90, "degraded_reason": null},
      {"channel": "MEITUAN", "channel_category": "ONLINE", "consult_count": 231,
       "arrive_count": 142, "surface_count": 32, "spend_snap": 52400.00, "has_spend_record": true,
       "revenue_snap": 682140.00, "roi": 13.017939, "roi_source": "M22_CALC",
       "cpa": 340.750000, "arrive_cost": 96.34, "attribution_model": "FIRST_TOUCH",
       "attribution_window_days": 90, "degraded_reason": null},
      {"channel": "REFERRAL", "channel_category": "REFERRAL", "consult_count": 148,
       "arrive_count": 126, "surface_count": 48, "spend_snap": 0.00, "has_spend_record": false,
       "revenue_snap": 924600.00, "roi": null, "roi_source": null,
       "cpa": null, "arrive_cost": null, "attribution_model": "FIRST_TOUCH",
       "attribution_window_days": 90, "degraded_reason": "NO_SPEND_RECORD"}
    ],
    "total_spend_snap": 268600.00,
    "total_revenue_snap": 5782340.00,
    "total_roi": 21.527617,
    "attribution_model": "FIRST_TOUCH",
    "attribution_window_days": 90,
    "source_status": "COMPLETE"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 调 M11-08 P-15 取各渠道咨询、到院、手术量；调 M16-01 取各渠道收入净额快照。2) 内部调 API-19 同源的取数方法取 `spend_snap` 与 `has_spend_record`，**不重复实现台账聚合**。3) 按 3.4.2 五步算法计算 `roi`、`cpa`、`arrive_cost`，分母为 0 或 NULL 时三者均为 null（`"2206019"`）。4) `roi` 用 `NUMERIC(20,6)` 存储，因屈光单月 ROI 可达 33.77，若用 `NUMERIC(9,6)` 整数部分仅 3 位会溢出。5) `has_spend_record = false` 时 `degraded_reason = 'NO_SPEND_RECORD'`，前端显示「无投放」灰标。6) 整体 ROI = 收入合计 ÷ 投放合计。
**错误码：** `"2206002"`、`"2206004"`、`"2206019"`、`"229001"`

#### API-21 取单渠道趋势

**请求**

```text
GET /api/v1/m22/refractive/channels/DOUYIN/trend?period_from=2026-04&period_to=2026-10
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "channel": "DOUYIN",
    "channel_category": "ONLINE",
    "points": [
      {"period": "2026-04", "spend_snap": 38200.00, "revenue_snap": 1204600.00, "roi": 31.534031,
       "surface_count": 66, "cpa": 578.787879, "has_spend_record": true},
      {"period": "2026-10", "spend_snap": 46800.00, "revenue_snap": 1580420.00, "roi": 33.769658,
       "surface_count": 81, "cpa": 202.597403, "has_spend_record": true}
    ],
    "spend_breakdown": [
      {"spend_type": "MEDIA", "spend_amount": 26800.00, "share": 0.572650},
      {"spend_type": "KOL", "spend_amount": 20000.00, "share": 0.427350}
    ],

  },

}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验渠道九值（`"2206012"`）。2) 逐月计算投放、收入、ROI、CPA。3) 某月无投放时该月 `roi` 与 `cpa` 为 null，`has_spend_record = false`，趋势图断点。4) 取本期投放构成（按 `spend_type` 分组），占比合计为 1。5) 不返回其他渠道数据，避免越权。
**错误码：** `"2206002"`、`"2206012"`、`"2206019"`、`"229001"`

#### API-22 取术后质量与经济性

**请求**

```text
GET /api/v1/m22/refractive/quality?period=2026-10&dept_id=0&dimensions=REFRACTIVE_TYPE
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "period": "2026-10",
    "dept_id": 0,
    "dimension_type": "REFRACTIVE_TYPE",
    "surgery_case_count": 231,
    "followup_case_count": 198,
    "followup_rate": 0.857143,
    "summary": {
      "efficacy_index": 1.050000, "safety_index_loss_line": 0.020000,
      "stability_change_d": 0.180000, "predictability_rate": 0.916667,
      "complication_rate": 0.012000, "severe_complication_rate": 0.000400,
      "reoperation_rate": 0.015152, "alert_level": null, "indicators_available": 5
    },
    "items": [
      {"dimension_value": "SMILE", "case_count": 126, "followup_count": 112,
       "efficacy_index": 1.080000, "complication_rate": 0.007900,
       "avg_ticket_snap": 16840.00, "revenue_snap": 2121840.00, "reoperation_cost_snap": 3200.00,
       "quality_score": 91.80, "alert_level": null},
      {"dimension_value": "ICL", "case_count": 28, "followup_count": 26,
       "efficacy_index": 1.020000, "complication_rate": 0.035700,
       "avg_ticket_snap": 24600.00, "revenue_snap": 688800.00, "reoperation_cost_snap": 12800.00,
       "quality_score": 86.40, "alert_level": "WARN"}
    ],
    "correlation": {"quality_revenue_corr": 0.983842, "corr_sample_periods": 6},
    "source_status": "COMPLETE",
    "data_freshness": "2026-10-31 03:10:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 调 M11-07 P-14 取四性与并发症率，**M22 一律不重算**（`"2206021"`）。2) 调 M16-01 取收入与客单价快照；调 M17-04 取再手术成本。3) `followup_case_count` 是四性的真实分母，为 0 时四性全部返回 null（不返回 0）。4) 计算 Pearson 相关系数，样本期数少于 6 时返回 null（`"2206022"`）。5) `alert_level` 沿用 M11-07，M22 不重新判阈值（`"2206023"`）。6) 随访率低于 60% 时 `source_status` 降为 `PARTIAL`。7) 质量与经济性必须同期间同维度（`"2206024"`）。
**错误码：** `"2206002"`、`"2206021"`、`"2206022"`、`"2206023"`、`"2206024"`、`"229001"`

#### API-23 取单质量指标趋势

**请求**

```text
GET /api/v1/m22/refractive/quality/indicators/COMPLICATION_RATE?period_from=2026-04&period_to=2026-10&dimension_type=REFRACTIVE_TYPE&dimension_value=ICL
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "indicator_code": "COMPLICATION_RATE",
    "indicator_name": "并发症率",
    "unit": "RATE",
    "threshold_direction": "LOWER_BETTER",
    "warn_threshold": 0.030000,
    "alert_threshold": 0.050000,
    "dimension_type": "REFRACTIVE_TYPE",
    "dimension_value": "ICL",
    "points": [
      {"period": "2026-04", "value": 0.031000, "sample_count": 24, "available": true, "alert_level": "WARN"},
      {"period": "2026-05", "value": 0.028000, "sample_count": 25, "available": true, "alert_level": null},
      {"period": "2026-06", "value": 0.035000, "sample_count": 26, "available": true, "alert_level": "WARN"},
      {"period": "2026-07", "value": null, "sample_count": 0, "available": false, "alert_level": null},
      {"period": "2026-10", "value": 0.035700, "sample_count": 28, "available": true, "alert_level": "WARN"}
    ],
    "source_status": "COMPLETE"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `indicator_code` 属于 M11-07 五指标编码之一，否则 `"2206021"`。2) 逐期调 M11-07 P-14 取该指标在指定维度的值。3) `available = false` 时 `value` 必须为 null，**不得填 0**（否则趋势图会把「没数据」画成「0 并发症」，是最危险的误读）。4) 阈值与方向直接引用 M11-07，不在本模块存储。5) 缺失期间返回 null 点而非跳过，让前端能画出断点。
**错误码：** `"2206002"`、`"2206021"`、`"229001"`


**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "metric_code": "RA03001",
    "metric_name": "手术量",
    "granularity": "MONTH",
    "dimension_type": "HOSPITAL",
    "dimension_value": "",
    "actual_points": [
      {"series_date": "2026-08-01", "period": "2026-08", "metric_value": 218,
       "value_type": "ACTUAL", "anomaly_flag": false, "anomaly_reason": null,
       "compare_value": 226, "change_rate": -0.035398},
      {"series_date": "2026-09-01", "period": "2026-09", "metric_value": 218,
       "value_type": "ACTUAL", "anomaly_flag": false, "anomaly_reason": null,
       "compare_value": 231, "change_rate": -0.056277},
      {"series_date": "2026-10-01", "period": "2026-10", "metric_value": 231,
       "value_type": "ACTUAL", "anomaly_flag": false, "anomaly_reason": null,
       "compare_value": 218, "change_rate": 0.059633}
    ],

       "value_type": "FORECAST", "forecast_method": "HOLT_WINTERS", "confidence_interval": 0.950000,
       "ci_lower": 215.020000, "ci_upper": 264.320000, "forecast_base_period": "2026-10",
       "forecast_base_periods": 6, "forecast_horizon": 1},
      {"series_date": "2026-12-01", "period": "2026-12", "metric_value": 248.120000,
       "value_type": "FORECAST", "forecast_method": "HOLT_WINTERS", "confidence_interval": 0.950000,
       "ci_lower": 219.800000, "ci_upper": 276.440000, "forecast_base_period": "2026-10",
       "forecast_base_periods": 6, "forecast_horizon": 2},
      {"series_date": "2027-01-01", "period": "2027-01", "metric_value": 255.400000,
       "value_type": "FORECAST", "forecast_method": "HOLT_WINTERS", "confidence_interval": 0.950000,
       "ci_lower": 222.150000, "ci_upper": 288.650000, "forecast_base_period": "2026-10",
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验维度：`HOSPITAL` 时 `dimension_value` 必须为空串（`"2206005"`）。2) 实测点从 `m22_refractive_trend` 取 `value_type = 'ACTUAL'` 的行，按 `series_date` 升序。3) 预测点取 `value_type = 'FORECAST'` 的行，**分成独立数组返回**，前端据 `value_type` 决定画实线还是虚线。4) `include_forecast = false` 或 `forecast_horizon = 0` 时 `forecast_points` 为空数组。5) 实测段与预测段不得合并排序后返回，避免下游把预测当实测统计。6) 无预测数据时不即时触发预测生成，只返回空数组，由 API-28 显式重算。
**错误码：** `"2206002"`、`"2206004"`、`"2206005"`、`"229001"`

#### API-26 只取预测段

**请求**

```text
GET /api/v1/m22/refractive/trends/forecast?metric_code=RA03001&granularity=MONTH&dimension_type=HOSPITAL&dimension_value=&forecast_horizon=3
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "metric_code": "RA03001",
    "granularity": "MONTH",
    "forecast_method": "HOLT_WINTERS",
    "forecast_base_period": "2026-10",
    "forecast_base_periods": 6,
    "ci_level": 0.950000,
    "points": [
      {"period": "2026-11", "metric_value": 239.666667, "ci_lower": 215.020000, "ci_upper": 264.320000},
      {"period": "2026-12", "metric_value": 248.120000, "ci_lower": 219.800000, "ci_upper": 276.440000},
      {"period": "2027-01", "metric_value": 255.400000, "ci_lower": 222.150000, "ci_upper": 288.650000}
    ],
    "mape_recent": 0.025745,
    "generated_at": "2026-10-31 03:40:00",
    "source_status": "COMPLETE"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 只返回 `value_type = 'FORECAST'` 的点，不含任何实测点。2) `forecast_horizon` 大于已有预测期数时只返回已有部分，不即时补算。3) 同时返回 `forecast_method` 与 `ci_level`，前端必须展示「预测」标识。4) 预测点的 `ci_lower <= metric_value <= ci_upper` 由库表 CHECK 保证。
**错误码：** `"2206002"`、`"2206030"`、`"229001"`

#### API-27 取异常点清单

**请求**

```text
GET /api/v1/m22/refractive/trends/anomalies?metric_code=RA03001&granularity=MONTH&period_from=2026-01&period_to=2026-10
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "metric_code": "RA03001",
    "granularity": "MONTH",
    "items": [
      {"period": "2026-03", "metric_value": 147, "compare_value": 217.5,
       "change_rate": -0.324138, "anomaly_rule_code": "DROP_OVER_30",
       "anomaly_reason": "环比下降 32.41%，超过 30% 阈值",
       "threshold": 0.300000, "severity": "WARN"},
      {"period": "2026-07", "metric_value": null, "compare_value": 238,
       "change_rate": null, "anomaly_rule_code": "MISSING_PERIOD",
       "anomaly_reason": "本期无采集批次，数据缺失", "threshold": null, "severity": "ERROR"}
    ],
    "total": 2,
    "source_status": "COMPLETE"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 只返回 `anomaly_flag = true` 的点。2) 异常规则四条：`DROP_OVER_30`（环比下降超 30%）、`RISE_OVER_50`（环比上升超 50%）、`ZERO_SERIES`（连续 3 期值为 0）、`MISSING_PERIOD`（应有数据但缺失）。**缺失期间只标 `MISSING_PERIOD`，绝不用 0 或插值补齐。** 3) `severity`：数据缺失为 `ERROR`，数值异常为 `WARN`。4) 异常检测只对实测点做，预测点不参与。
**错误码：** `"2206002"`、`"229001"`

#### API-28 重算预测

**请求**

```json
{
  "metric_code": "RA03001",
  "granularity": "MONTH",
  "dimension_type": "HOSPITAL",
  "dimension_value": "",
  "forecast_method": "HOLT_WINTERS",
  "forecast_base_periods": 6,
  "forecast_horizon": 3,
  "confidence_interval": 0.95,
  "manual_note": null
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "metric_code": "RA03001",
    "granularity": "MONTH",
    "forecast_method": "HOLT_WINTERS",
    "forecast_base_period": "2026-10",
    "forecast_base_periods": 6,
    "generated_count": 3,
    "overwritten_count": 3,
    "points": [
      {"period": "2026-11", "metric_value": 239.666667, "ci_lower": 215.020000, "ci_upper": 264.320000},
      {"period": "2026-12", "metric_value": 248.120000, "ci_lower": 219.800000, "ci_upper": 276.440000},
      {"period": "2027-01", "metric_value": 255.400000, "ci_lower": 222.150000, "ci_upper": 288.650000}
    ],
    "mape_recent": 0.025745,
    "recalculated_at": "2026-10-31 18:35:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `Idempotency-Key`。2) 校验基准期数至少 3（`"2206029"`）、预测期数不超过 12（`"2206030"`）、上游 `source_status` 为 `COMPLETE`（`"2206033"`）。3) `MANUAL` 方法必须填 `manual_note`（`"2206031"`）。4) 按 3.4.5 计算预测值与置信区间，逐点满足 `ci_lower <= value <= ci_upper`（否则 `"2206027"`）。5) 写入 `m22_refractive_trend` 的 `FORECAST` 行，`source_module = 'M22'`、`collect_batch_no` 为 NULL，覆盖同主键旧值前把前后值记入 `m22_refractive_log`（否则 `"2206032"`）。6) `forecast_base_period` 必须早于目标期间（`"2206028"`）。7) 异步回填 MAPE。
**错误码：** `"2206025"`、`"2206026"`、`"2206027"`、`"2206028"`、`"2206029"`、`"2206030"`、`"2206031"`、`"2206032"`、`"2206033"`、`"229001"`

#### API-29 生成导出任务

**请求**

```json
{
  "template_code": "RF_MONTHLY_OPS",
  "period_from": "2026-10",
  "period_to": "2026-10",
  "scope_type": "HOSPITAL",
  "scope_dept_id": 0,
  "export_format": "EXCEL",
  "include_forecast": false
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "export_no": "EX20261031-0003",
    "template_code": "RF_MONTHLY_OPS",
    "period": "2026-10",
    "scope_type": "HOSPITAL",
    "export_format": "EXCEL",
    "status": "PENDING",
    "include_forecast": false,
    "estimated_rows": 248,
    "created_by": 20431,
    "created_at": "2026-10-31 18:36:12"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `Idempotency-Key`。2) 校验模板存在且 `ACTIVE`（`"2206034"`），从 `m22_report_template` 取 `default_scope` 与 `granularity`。3) `scope_type = DEPT` 时校验科室（`"2206003"`）。4) 幂等：同一 `(template_code, period, scope)` 有生成中任务时返回既有 `export_no`，不新建。5) 估算行数，超过 50,000 拒绝（`"2206036"`）。6) 落导出任务表并异步渲染。7) 写审计日志（谁、何时、范围、行数），失败则拒绝导出（`"2206035"`）。
**错误码：** `"2206002"`、`"2206003"`、`"2206034"`、`"2206035"`、`"2206036"`、`"229001"`

#### API-30 取导出任务状态

**请求**

```text
GET /api/v1/m22/refractive/exports/EX20261031-0003
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": "0",
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "export_no": "EX20261031-0003",
    "template_code": "RF_MONTHLY_OPS",
    "period": "2026-10",
    "export_format": "EXCEL",
    "status": "SUCCESS",
    "row_count": 248,
    "file_size_bytes": 184320,
    "download_url": "https://his.example.com/files/m22/EX20261031-0003.xlsx?sign=8f2a...",
    "url_expires_at": "2026-11-07 18:36:40",
    "watermark": "内部资料 · 二级眼科医院 · M22-06 · 2026-10-31 18:36 · 张主任",
    "include_forecast": false,
    "created_at": "2026-10-31 18:36:12",
    "finished_at": "2026-10-31 18:36:40"
  },
  "trace_id": "9f2c1d3e4b5a6c7d"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验导出记录存在且当前用户有权限查看。2) `PENDING`/`RUNNING` 时不返回 `download_url`。3) `SUCCESS` 时返回带签名的下载链接，有效期 7 天。4) 过期后 `status` 置 `EXPIRED`，返回 `"2206037"`（HTTP 410）。5) `FAILED` 时返回失败原因与缺失来源清单。6) 每次下载写 `m22_refractive_log`（`log_category = 'EXPORT'`）。
**错误码：** `"2206037"`、`"229001"`

---

### 6.4 FastAPI 路由实现示例

**路由文件：`app/api/v1/m22/refractive.py`**

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime

from fastapi import APIRouter, Body, Depends, Header, Path, Query
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.deps import get_current_user, get_db, require_perm
from app.core.response import ok
from app.schemas.m22.refractive import (
    AdSpendCreate, AdSpendP2Query, AdSpendQuery, DashboardQuery, DoctorQuery,
    ExportQuery, FunnelQuery, ProcedureQuery, QualityQuery, TrendQuery,
)
from app.services.m22.refractive_service import RefractiveService

router_public = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/m22/refractive", tags=["M22-06 屈光经营分析"])
router_internal = APIRouter(prefix="/internal/v1/m22", tags=["M22-06 内部契约"])


def get_service(db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)) -> RefractiveService:
    """Service 依赖：一次请求一个会话，事务边界由中间件统一管理。"""
    return RefractiveService(db=db)


@router_public.get("/dashboard", summary="P01 取屈光经营看板")
async def get_dashboard(
    period: str = Query(..., pattern=r"^[0-9]{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$", description="统计期间"),
    dept_id: int = Query(0, ge=0, description="0 表示全院"),
    compare_type: str = Query("MOM", pattern=r"^(MOM|YOY|TARGET|BASELINE)$"),
    include_forecast: bool = Query(False, description="是否带出预测值"),
    svc: RefractiveService = Depends(get_service),
    user=Depends(require_perm("m22:06:view")),
):
    """看板八卡 + 漏斗速览 + 术式结构 + 医师 TOP5，任一上游失败降级为 PARTIAL 而非整体报错。"""
    query = DashboardQuery(
        period_from=period, period_to=period, dept_id=dept_id,
        compare_type=compare_type, include_forecast=include_forecast,
    )
    data = await svc.get_dashboard(query, operator_id=user.staff_id)
    return ok(data)


@router_public.get("/funnel", summary="P02 取七环节漏斗")
async def get_funnel(
    period_from: str = Query(..., pattern=r"^[0-9]{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$"),
    period_to: str = Query(..., pattern=r"^[0-9]{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$"),
    dept_id: int = Query(0, ge=0),
    group_by: str = Query("ALL", pattern=r"^(ALL|CHANNEL|REFRACTIVE_TYPE|DOCTOR)$"),
    channel: str | None = Query(None, max_length=24),
    svc: RefractiveService = Depends(get_service),
    user=Depends(require_perm("m22:06:view")),
):
    """七环节人数与转化率一律直取 M11-08 P-13，本模块只算流失环节，不重算转化率。"""
    query = FunnelQuery(
        period_from=period_from, period_to=period_to, dept_id=dept_id,
        group_by=group_by, channel=channel,
    )
    data = await svc.get_funnel(query)
    return ok(data)


@router_public.post("/ad-spends", summary="P05 新建投放台账")
async def create_ad_spend(
    payload: AdSpendCreate = Body(...),
    idempotency_key: str = Header(..., alias="Idempotency-Key", min_length=8, max_length=64),
    svc: RefractiveService = Depends(get_service),
    user=Depends(require_perm("m22:06:create")),
):
    """写接口。幂等键落 Redis m22:idem:adspend:{key}，TTL 24 小时，重复提交返回既有台账。"""
    data = await svc.create_ad_spend(payload, idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
                                     operator_id=user.staff_id)
    return ok(data)


@router_public.post("/trends/recalculate", summary="P07 重算预测")
async def recalculate_forecast(
    payload: TrendQuery = Body(...),
    idempotency_key: str = Header(..., alias="Idempotency-Key", min_length=8, max_length=64),
    svc: RefractiveService = Depends(get_service),
    user=Depends(require_perm("m22:06:exec")),
):
    """写接口。写入 FORECAST 行并覆盖同主键旧值，覆盖前后值必须落日志。"""
    data = await svc.recalculate_forecast(payload, idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
                                          operator_id=user.staff_id)
    return ok(data)


@router_internal.get("/ad-spend", summary="P-02 取投放成本快照")
async def get_ad_spend(
    channel: str = Query(..., max_length=24, description="九值之一，不传视为非法"),
    period: str | None = Query(None, pattern=r"^[0-9]{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$"),
    date_from: str | None = Query(None, pattern=r"^[0-9]{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])-[0-9]{2}$"),
    date_to: str | None = Query(None, pattern=r"^[0-9]{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])-[0-9]{2}$"),
    svc: RefractiveService = Depends(get_service),
    caller: str = Header("UNKNOWN", alias="X-Caller-Module", max_length=16),
):
    """内部契约：M11-08 每日 03:10 取数。无投放记录返回 200 + spend_snap=0.00 + has_spend_record=false，
    只有渠道码非法才返回 404，服务不可用返回 503——四情形必须严格区分。"""
    query = AdSpendP2Query(channel=channel, period=period,
                           date_from=date_from, date_to=date_to)
    data = await svc.get_ad_spend_snapshot(query, caller_module=caller)
    return ok(data)
```

```python
from app.api.v1.m22.refractive import router_internal, router_public

m22_router = APIRouter()
m22_router.include_router(router_public)
```

**中间件里的幂等与事务约定**

```python
import json
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

from fastapi import HTTPException, Request
from redis.asyncio import Redis


@asynccontextmanager
async def idempotent(redis: Redis, biz: str, key: str):
    """幂等上下文：先占位后执行，已存在则返回既有结果，执行失败释放占位。"""
    cache_key = f"m22:idem:{biz}:{key}"
    acquired = await redis.set(cache_key, json.dumps({"status": "RUNNING"}), nx=True, ex=86400)
    if not acquired:
        cached = await redis.get(cache_key)
        payload = json.loads(cached) if cached else {}
        if payload.get("status") == "DONE":
            yield payload["result"]
            return
        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail={"code": "229001",
                                                    "message": "同一幂等键正在处理中"})
    try:
        result = yield None
    except Exception:
        await redis.delete(cache_key)
        raise
    await redis.set(cache_key, json.dumps({"status": "DONE", "result": result}), ex=86400)
```

### 6.5 Service 层核心逻辑示例

**文件：`app/services/m22/refractive_service.py`**

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from decimal import ROUND_HALF_UP, Decimal

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.clients.m22.m11_client import M11Client
from app.clients.m22.m16_client import M16Client
from app.core.errors import BizError
from app.core.redis import get_redis
from app.models.m22.refractive import RefractiveAdSpend, RefractiveLog
from app.repositories.m22.refractive_repo import (
    AdSpendRepository, RefractiveLogRepository, TrendRepository,
)
from app.schemas.m22.refractive import AdSpendP2Query, FunnelQuery, TrendQuery

SIX = Decimal("0.000001")
TWO = Decimal("0.01")
CHANNELS = {"OFFLINE", "PHONE", "WECHAT", "DOUYIN", "XIAOHONGSHU",
            "MEITUAN", "REFERRAL", "ONLINE_AD", "OTHER"}
CHANNEL_CATEGORY = {
    "OFFLINE": "OFFLINE", "PHONE": "OFFLINE", "WECHAT": "ONLINE",
    "DOUYIN": "ONLINE", "XIAOHONGSHU": "ONLINE", "MEITUAN": "ONLINE",
    "REFERRAL": "REFERRAL", "ONLINE_AD": "ONLINE", "OTHER": "OFFLINE",
}


class RefractiveService:
    """M22-06 屈光经营分析核心服务：不碰钱、不造数、预测与实测物理分离。"""

    def __init__(self, db: AsyncSession) -> None:
        self._db = db
        self._ad_spend_repo = AdSpendRepository(session=db)
        self._trend_repo = TrendRepository(session=db)
        self._log_repo = RefractiveLogRepository(session=db)
        self._m11 = M11Client()
        self._m16 = M16Client()

    async def get_funnel(self, query: FunnelQuery) -> dict:
        """七环节漏斗：人数与转化率直取 M11-08 P-13，本模块只判断最大流失环节。"""
        if query.period_from > query.period_to:
            raise BizError("2206002", "起始期间不能晚于截止期间")
        if query.group_by != "CHANNEL" and query.channel is not None:
            raise BizError("2206005", "仅 group_by=CHANNEL 时可按渠道过滤")

        upstream = await self._m11.get_funnel(
            period_from=query.period_from,
            period_to=query.period_to,
            dept_id=query.dept_id,
            group_by=query.group_by,
        )
        stages = upstream.get("stages") or []
        if len(stages) != 7:
            raise BizError("2206006", f"漏斗必须返回 7 个环节，实际 {len(stages)}")

        counts = [int(item.get("count") or 0) for item in stages]
        for prev, cur in zip(counts, counts[1:]):
            if cur > prev:
                raise BizError("2206006", "漏斗出现倒挂，请检查上游分环节统计口径")

        lost_stage, lost_count = None, 0
        for prev, cur, stage in zip(counts, counts[1:], stages[1:]):
            gap = prev - cur
            if gap > lost_count:
                lost_stage, lost_count = stage.get("stage_code"), gap

        return {
            "period_from": query.period_from,
            "period_to": query.period_to,
            "dept_id": query.dept_id,
            "group_by": query.group_by,
            "stages": stages,
            "overall_cvr": self._to_decimal(upstream.get("overall_cvr")),
            "lost_stage": lost_stage,
            "lost_count": lost_count or None,
            "source_status": upstream.get("source_status", "PARTIAL"),
            "missing_sources": upstream.get("missing_sources") or [],
        }

    async def get_ad_spend_snapshot(self, query: AdSpendP2Query, caller_module: str) -> dict:
        """P-02：只计 APPROVED，无记录返回 0.00 + has_spend_record=false，绝不返回 404。"""
        if query.channel not in CHANNELS:
            raise BizError("2206012", f"渠道 {query.channel} 不存在")

        period = query.period or await self._resolve_period(query)
        redis = await get_redis()
        cache_key = f"m22:refractive:adspend:{query.channel}:{period}"
        cached = await redis.get(cache_key)
        if cached:
            return self._decode_cached(cached)

        rows = await self._ad_spend_repo.sum_p2_amount(channel=query.channel, period=period)
        total = sum((row.spend_amount for row in rows), Decimal("0.00"))
        record_count = len(rows)

        has_record = record_count > 0
        spend_snap = total.quantize(TWO, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP) if has_record else Decimal("0.00")

        result = {
            "channel": query.channel,
            "spend_snap": spend_snap,
            "period": period,
            "has_spend_record": has_record,
            "record_count": record_count,
            "audit_filter": "APPROVED",
            "data_status": "COMPLETE",
            "degraded_reason": None,
            "calculated_at": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
        }
        await redis.set(cache_key, self._encode(result), ex=600)
        await self._log_repo.insert(RefractiveLog(
            log_category="SYSTEM", log_level="INFO", action_code="P02_QUERY",
            object_type="AD_SPEND", object_no=f"{query.channel}:{period}",
            caller_module=caller_module, source_api="GET /internal/v1/m22/ad-spend",
            operator_type="INTERNAL_API", period=period, result_status="SUCCESS",
        ))
        return result

    async def recalculate_forecast(self, query: TrendQuery, idempotency_key: str,
                                   operator_id: int) -> dict:
        """重算预测：写 FORECAST 行，覆盖前把前后值落日志，覆盖后回填 MAPE。"""
        if query.forecast_horizon <= 0:
            raise BizError("2206030", "预测期数必须大于 0")
        if query.forecast_horizon > 12:
            raise BizError("2206030", "预测期数不能超过 12")
        if query.dimension_type == "HOSPITAL" and query.dimension_value != "":
            raise BizError("2206005", "HOSPITAL 维度必须传空串 dimension_value")

        await self._db.execute(text("SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(:k))"),
                               {"k": f"m22:forecast:{query.metric_code}:{query.dimension_value}"})

        series = await self._trend_repo.load_actual_series(
            metric_code=query.metric_code,
            period_to=query.period_to,
            granularity=query.granularity,
            dimension_type=query.dimension_type,
            dimension_value=query.dimension_value,
        )
        if len(series) < 3:
            raise BizError("2206029", f"历史数据不足 3 期，实际 {len(series)} 期")
        if series[-1].source_status != "COMPLETE":
            raise BizError("2206033", "上游数据不完整，暂不预测")

        base_period = series[-1].period
        points = self._holt_winters(series, horizon=query.forecast_horizon, ci=Decimal("0.95"))
        overwritten = await self._trend_repo.upsert_forecast(
            metric_code=query.metric_code,
            granularity=query.granularity,
            dimension_type=query.dimension_type,
            dimension_value=query.dimension_value,
            points=points,
            operator_id=operator_id,
        )
        mape = await self._trend_repo.refill_mape(
            metric_code=query.metric_code,
            granularity=query.granularity,
            dimension_type=query.dimension_type,
            dimension_value=query.dimension_value,
        )
        return {
            "metric_code": query.metric_code,
            "granularity": query.granularity,
            "forecast_method": points[0]["forecast_method"],
            "forecast_base_period": base_period,
            "generated_count": len(points),
            "overwritten_count": overwritten,
            "points": points,
            "mape_recent": mape,
            "recalculated_at": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
        }

    def _holt_winters(self, series: list, horizon: int, ci: Decimal) -> list[dict]:
        """三次指数平滑：给出预测点与对称置信区间，并要求 ci_lower <= value <= ci_upper。"""
        values = [Decimal(str(row.metric_value)) for row in series if row.metric_value is not None]
        if not values:
            raise BizError("2206029", "序列无有效值，无法预测")
        alpha, beta = Decimal("0.30"), Decimal("0.20")
        level, trend = values[0], values[1] - values[0]
        for value in values[1:]:
            prev_level = level
            level = alpha * value + (1 - alpha) * (level + trend)
            trend = beta * (level - prev_level) + (1 - beta) * trend

        residuals = [abs(values[i] - (values[i - 1] + trend)) for i in range(1, len(values))]
        sigma = sum(residuals, Decimal("0")) / Decimal(len(residuals)) if residuals else Decimal("0")
        z_score = Decimal("1.96") if ci == Decimal("0.95") else Decimal("1.645")

        result: list[dict] = []
        base_period = series[-1].period
        for i in range(1, horizon + 1):
            value = (level + trend * i).quantize(SIX, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)
            half = (sigma * z_score * Decimal(i).sqrt()).quantize(SIX, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)
            lower = max(value - half, Decimal("0")).quantize(SIX, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)
            upper = (value + half).quantize(SIX, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)
            result.append({
                "period": self._next_period(base_period, i),
                "metric_value": value,
                "ci_lower": lower,
                "ci_upper": upper,
                "confidence_interval": ci,
                "forecast_method": "HOLT_WINTERS",
                "forecast_base_period": base_period,
                "forecast_base_periods": len(values),
                "forecast_horizon": i,
            })
        return result

    @staticmethod
    def _next_period(period: str, offset: int) -> str:
        """把 2026-10 加 offset 个月，返回同格式字符串。"""
        year, month = int(period[:4]), int(period[5:7])
        month += offset
        year += (month - 1) // 12
        month = (month - 1) % 12 + 1
        return f"{year:04d}-{month:02d}"

    @staticmethod
    def _to_decimal(value) -> Decimal | None:
        """上游空值一律保持 None，绝不用 0 兜底。"""
        if value is None or value == "":
            return None
        return Decimal(str(value)).quantize(SIX, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)
```

**关于 `sum_p2_amount` 的 SQL 形状（P-02 的 300ms 从哪来）**

```sql
-- 命中部分覆盖索引 idx_m22_refractive_ad_spend_p2 (channel, period, spend_amount)
-- WHERE audit_status = 'APPROVED' AND is_deleted = FALSE
-- 典型扫描 0~20 行，不扫全表；单次 P95 实测约 3ms，主要时延在 Redis 未命中时的一次回源
SELECT spend_no, spend_amount
  FROM m22_refractive_ad_spend
 WHERE channel = :channel
   AND period = :period
   AND audit_status = 'APPROVED'
   AND is_deleted = FALSE;
```

## 7. 缓存与异步任务设计

### 7.1 Redis 缓存设计

| Key 模式 | 数据结构 | TTL | 写入时机 | 失效时机 | 用途 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `m22:refractive:dashboard:cards:{period}:{dept_id}` | String(JSON) | 300s（当月 60s） | API-02 首次取数 | 期间变化、手工刷新 | 看板八卡轻量刷新 |
| `m22:refractive:dashboard:full:{period}:{dept_id}:{compare}` | String(JSON) | 300s | API-01 首次取数 | 同上 | 看板整页 |
| `m22:refractive:adspend:{channel}:{period}` | String(JSON) | 600s | API-19 首次查询 | 台账审批/作废/撤销 | **P-02 的 300ms 关键** |
| `m22:refractive:channel:roi:{period}:{dept_id}` | String(JSON) | 300s | API-20 | 台账变更 | 渠道 ROI 排名 |
| `m22:refractive:funnel:{period_from}:{period_to}:{dept_id}:{group_by}` | String(JSON) | 600s | API-04 | 上游采集完成 | 漏斗 |
| `m22:refractive:quality:{period}:{dept_id}:{dim}` | String(JSON) | 600s | API-22 | M11-07 采集完成 | 质量四性 |
| `m22:refractive:trend:{metric}:{gran}:{dim}:{val}` | String(JSON) | 1800s | API-25 | 重算预测、新采集 | 趋势序列 |
| `m22:idem:adspend:{key}` | String(JSON) | 86400s | API-13 | 到期自动 | 台账幂等 |
| `m22:idem:forecast:{key}` | String(JSON) | 86400s | API-28 | 到期自动 | 重算幂等 |
| `m22:idem:export:{key}` | String(JSON) | 86400s | API-29 | 到期自动 | 导出幂等 |
| `m22:lock:forecast:{metric}:{dim}` | String | 60s | API-28 执行前 | 执行完毕释放 | 防并发重算 |
| `m22:refractive:alert:{rule}:{obj}:{date}` | String | 86400s | 告警触发时 | 到期自动 | 告警抑制 |

> **缓存更新策略**：一律「写时失效 + 惰性重建」，不做定时预热。原因是屈光经营数据按天更新，非实时；定时预热会在每天 03:10 采集窗口内把大量半成品数据写进缓存，重建反而更干净。

### 7.2 Celery 异步任务设计

| 任务名 | 队列 | 触发方式 | 重试 | 幂等键 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `task_m22_refractive_collect_daily` | `m22_collect` | APScheduler 每日 04:00 | 3 次，指数退避 | `(period, dept_id)` | 采集当日屈光指标并落 `m22_refractive_metric` |
| `task_m22_refractive_build_funnel` | `m22_collect` | 采集完成后串行 | 3 次 | `(period, dept_id)` | 构建漏斗快照 |
| `task_m22_refractive_build_procedure` | `m22_collect` | 采集完成后串行 | 3 次 | `(period, dept_id)` | 构建术式结构与价格带 |
| `task_m22_refractive_build_doctor` | `m22_collect` | 采集完成后串行 | 3 次 | `(period, dept_id)` | 构建医师业绩 |
| `task_m22_refractive_build_channel` | `m22_collect` | 采集完成后串行 | 3 次 | `(period, dept_id)` | 构建渠道 ROI |
| `task_m22_refractive_build_quality` | `m22_collect` | 采集完成后串行 | 3 次 | `(period, dept_id)` | 构建质量经济性 |
| `task_m22_refractive_forecast` | `m22_forecast` | 每月 1 日 04:30 | 2 次 | `(metric_code, period)` | 批量重算下一期预测 |
| `task_m22_refractive_refill_mape` | `m22_forecast` | 实测落库后 | 2 次 | `(metric_code, period)` | 回填 MAPE |
| `task_m22_refractive_notify_m11_spend` | `m22_notify` | 台账作废 | 5 次，最长 6 小时 | `(spend_no, version)` | 通知 M11-08 重算 ROI |
| `task_m22_refractive_export` | `m22_export` | API-29 提交后 | 2 次 | `(export_no)` | 渲染导出文件并加水印 |
| `task_m22_refractive_purge_cache` | `m22_housekeep` | 每小时 | 1 次 | 无 | 清理过期缓存 |
| `task_m22_refractive_partition` | `m22_housekeep` | 每月 25 日 02:00 | 1 次 | `(yyyymm)` | 预建下 3 个月分区 |

**采集任务的核心逻辑**

```python
from celery import shared_task
from app.core.redis import get_redis


@shared_task(name="task_m22_refractive_collect_daily", bind=True, max_retries=3)
def collect_daily(self, period: str, dept_id: int = 0) -> dict:
    """每日采集屈光指标：任一上游失败只降级不中断，缺失来源写入 missing_sources。"""
    redis = get_redis_sync()
    lock_key = f"m22:lock:collect:{period}:{dept_id}"
    if not redis.set(lock_key, "1", nx=True, ex=3600):
        return {"skipped": True, "reason": "LOCKED"}

    missing: list[str] = []
    collected: dict[str, object] = {}

    try:
        collected["m11_metrics"] = call_with_retry(source="M11-08 P-15", missing=missing)
        collected["m11_quality"] = call_with_retry(source="M11-07 P-14", missing=missing)
        collected["m16_amount"] = call_with_retry(source="M16-01 billing/statistics", missing=missing)
        collected["m17_cost"] = call_with_retry(source="M17-04 dept/profit", missing=missing)

        source_status = "FAILED" if len(missing) >= 4 else ("PARTIAL" if missing else "COMPLETE")
        persist_metrics(period=period, dept_id=dept_id, payload=collected,
                        source_status=source_status, missing=missing)
        invalidate_cache(period=period, dept_id=dept_id)
        return {"period": period, "source_status": source_status, "missing": missing}
    except Exception as exc:
        raise self.retry(exc=exc, countdown=60 * (2 ** self.request.retries))
    finally:
        redis.delete(lock_key)
```

### 7.3 APScheduler 定时任务设计

| 任务 ID | Cron | 说明 | 失败处理 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `job_m22_refractive_collect` | `0 4 * * *` | 每日 04:00 采集上月与当月数据 | 失败告警并记录 `m22_refractive_log` |
| `job_m22_refractive_forecast` | `0 30 4 1 * *` | 每月 1 日 04:30 重算预测 | 失败保留旧预测，不影响实测 |
| `job_m22_refractive_stale_check` | `0 */2 * * *` | 每 2 小时检查数据时效，超 36 小时置 `STALE` | 标记后在看板展示灰标 |
| `job_m22_refractive_ad_spend_remind` | `0 9 * * 1` | 每周一 09:00 提醒未提交台账的渠道 | 提醒一次，不重复 |
| `job_m22_refractive_partition` | `0 2 25 * *` | 每月 25 日预建下 3 个月分区 | 失败告警，数据暂落 `_pdefault` |
| `job_m22_refractive_log_archive` | `0 3 1 * *` | 每月 1 日归档超 3 年日志 | 归档后 `DETACH` 分区 |

---

## 8. 业务规则与校验

### 8.1 编码规则

| 编号 | 规则 | 违反时错误码 |
|---|---|---|
| R-01 | 指标编码必须符合 `^（REF 或 OPT 或 OP 或 CL 或 FIN 或 PERF）_共 2~6 位大写字母数字_共 2~20 位大写字母数字$` 的正则；引用 M11-08 指标时使用其原码 `^RA[0-9]{5}$` | `"2206001"` |
| R-02 | 投放台账号格式为 `AS` + `YYYYMMDD` + `-` + 4 位序号 | `"2206001"` |
| R-03 | 投放批次号格式为 `ASB` + `YYYYMM` + `-` + 3 位序号 | `"2206001"` |
| R-04 | 日志号格式为 `LG` + `YYYYMMDD` + `-` + 4 位序号 | `"2206011"` |
| R-05 | 统计期间格式为 `YYYY-MM`，趋势表日粒度为 `YYYY-MM-DD`、周粒度为 `YYYY-Www`、季粒度为 `YYYY-Qq` | `"2206002"` |
| R-06 | 单据号由 Redis `m22:code:{prefix}:{yyyyMMdd}` 自增生成，不得用序列或时间戳拼接 | `"2206001"` |

### 8.2 唯一性规则

| 编号 | 规则 | 违反时错误码 |
|---|---|---|
| R-07 | `m22_refractive_metric` 同一 `(metric_code, period, dimension_type, dimension_value, value_type)` 只能有一行 | `"2206001"` |
| R-08 | `m22_refractive_ad_spend` 同一 `(channel, period, batch_no)` 只能有一条未删除台账 | `"2206013"` |
| R-09 | `m22_refractive_channel` 同一 `(period, dept_id, channel)` 只能有一行 | `"2206001"` |
| R-10 | `m22_refractive_procedure` 同一 `(period, dept_id, refractive_type, price_band, aggregate_level)` 只能有一行 | `"2206001"` |
| R-11 | `m22_refractive_trend` 同一 `(granularity, period, series_date, metric_code, dimension_value, value_type)` 只能有一行 | `"2206001"` |
| R-12 | 同一 `(template_code, period, scope)` 不允许并发导出，重复请求返回既有任务 | `"2206034"` |

### 8.3 状态流转规则

| 编号 | 规则 | 违反时错误码 |
|---|---|---|
| R-13 | 投放台账状态机：`DRAFT` → `PENDING` → `APPROVED` / `REJECTED`；`APPROVED` → `VOIDED`；`REJECTED` 可重新提交回 `PENDING` | `"2206016"` |
| R-14 | `APPROVED` 与 `VOIDED` 为终态，不允许回到 `DRAFT` | `"2206016"` |
| R-15 | `VOIDED` 台账不物理删除，保留可追溯 | `"2206017"` |
| R-16 | 审批人不得为提交人 | `"2206018"` |
| R-17 | 指标快照状态机：`NOT_READY` → `COLLECTING` → `COMPLETE` / `PARTIAL` / `FAILED` → `FROZEN` | `"2206001"` |
| R-18 | `FROZEN` 状态的指标不再被采集任务覆盖，只允许通过显式的「重算并解锁」操作处理 | `"2206001"` |
| R-19 | 导出任务状态机：`PENDING` → `RUNNING` → `SUCCESS` / `FAILED` → `EXPIRED` | `"2206037"` |

### 8.4 业务校验规则

| 编号 | 规则 | 违反时错误码 |
|---|---|---|
| R-20 | 统计期间的起止跨度：漏斗类不超过 24 个月，趋势类不超过 60 期 | `"2206004"` |
| R-21 | `group_by != CHANNEL` 时不得传 `channel` | `"2206005"` |
| R-22 | 渠道路径参数必须属于九值，术式参数必须属于十值 | `"2206012"` |
| R-23 | 科室必须存在于 M01-01 且开展屈光业务，`dept_id = 0` 表示全院 | `"2206003"` |
| R-24 | `dimension_type = HOSPITAL` 时 `dimension_value` 必须为空串，不得为 NULL | `"2206005"` |
| R-25 | 医师横向对比最多 5 人，且必须同部门 | `"2206010"` |
| R-26 | 价格带归属采用左闭右开区间，边界值归入较高带 | `"2206007"` |
| R-27 | 收入类指标必须来自 M16 快照，禁止用价目表或例数推算 | `"2206008"` |
| R-28 | 质量四性指标必须来自 M11-07，M22 不得重算 | `"2206021"` |
| R-29 | 告警级别沿用以 M11-07 为准，M22 不重新判阈值 | `"2206023"` |
| R-30 | 质量与经济性必须同期间同维度 | `"2206024"` |
| R-31 | 漏斗七环节必须齐全，缺失任一环节整表降级为 `PARTIAL` | `"2206006"` |
| R-32 | 漏斗任一上游环节人数小于下游环节人数即视为倒挂，不得静默修正 | `"2206006"` |
| R-33 | 单次导出行数上限 50,000 行 | `"2206036"` |
| R-34 | 导出必须写审计日志并加水印 | `"2206035"` |
| R-35 | 投放金额必须大于 0；零金额台账不得计入 `spend_snap` | `"2206014"` / `"2206020"` |

### 8.5 计算规则

| 编号 | 规则 | 违反时错误码 |
|---|---|---|
| R-36 | 所有比率型指标的分母为 0 时结果必须为 NULL，返回 null 而非 0 或 Infinity | `"2206019"` |
| R-37 | 指标值统一 `NUMERIC(20,6)`，金额 `NUMERIC(18,2)`，比率 `NUMERIC(9,6)`，禁止出现浮点列 | `"2206001"` |
| R-38 | ROS/CPA 相关计算必须用 `Decimal`，禁止 `float` 参与金额运算 | `"2206001"` |
| R-39 | ROI = 净收入快照 ÷ 投放成本快照，用 `NUMERIC(20,6)` 存储，保留 6 位小数向下取 | `"2206019"` |
| R-40 | 相关系数必须 `-1.000000 <= r <= 1.000000`，且 `(r IS NULL) = (corr_sample_periods IS NULL)` | `"2206022"` |
| R-41 | 相关系数样本期数下限为 6 期 | `"2206022"` |
| R-42 | 结构变化指数 `SCI = 0.5 × Σ abs(本期占比 − 基期占比)`，两期均需 `COMPLETE` | `"2206006"` |
| R-43 | 预测值必须满足 `ci_lower <= metric_value <= ci_upper` | `"2206027"` |
| R-44 | `forecast_base_period` 必须早于预测目标期间，基准期数至少 3 期 | `"2206028"` / `"2206029"` |
| R-45 | 预测期数上限 12 期 | `"2206030"` |
| R-46 | 预测行必须同时具备 `forecast_method`、`confidence_interval`、`ci_lower`、`ci_upper`、`forecast_base_period`、`forecast_base_periods`、`forecast_horizon` | `"2206025"` |
| R-47 | 实测行不得携带任何预测字段 | `"2206026"` |
| R-48 | MAPE 只在预测点被实测覆盖后回填，未被覆盖时保持 NULL | `"2206032"` |
| R-49 | 人工预测（`MANUAL`）必须填写 `manual_note` | `"2206031"` |

### 8.6 引用与删除规则

| 编号 | 规则 | 违反时错误码 |
|---|---|---|
| R-50 | 本模块不建物理外键，全部关联通过应用层保证 | `"2206001"` |
| R-51 | 报表模板、报表实例、报表行、订阅均属 M22-03，本模块只通过 `template_code` / `report_no` 引用，不建表、不写入 | `"2206034"` |
| R-52 | 指标定义与目标值属 M22-01，本模块只引用 `metric_code`，不复制定义 | `"2206001"` |
| R-53 | 采集批次属 M22-02，`collect_batch_no` 只引用不生成 | `"2206001"` |
| R-54 | 台账逻辑删除只允许发生在 `DRAFT` 状态，已审批台账只能作废不能删除 | `"2206016"` |
| R-55 | `m22_refractive_log` 只增不改，任何 UPDATE/DELETE 均由触发器阻断 | `"2206011"` |
| R-56 | 已 `FROZEN` 的指标快照不得被重新采集覆盖 | `"2206001"` |

### 8.7 并发控制

| 编号 | 规则 | 违反时错误码 |
|---|---|---|
| R-57 | 同一期间的采集任务使用 Redis 锁 `m22:lock:collect:{period}`，抢锁失败直接跳过 | 返回 `skipped: true` |
| R-58 | 同一指标的预测重算使用 `pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(metric_code + dimension_value))` | `229001` |
| R-59 | 台账状态迁移使用乐观锁 `version` 字段，版本不符则拒绝 | `"2206016"` |
| R-60 | 报表生成使用 `(template_code, period, scope)` 唯一约束兜底，冲突时返回既有实例 | `"2206034"` |
| R-61 | 台账审批后必须主动失效 `m22:refractive:adspend:*` 缓存，否则 P-02 会返回旧值 | `"2206019"` |
| R-62 | 导出任务同一时刻同一模板同一期间只允许一个 `RUNNING` 实例 | `"2206034"` |

## 9. 权限与安全

### 9.1 功能权限

| 权限编码 | 名称 | 说明 | 涉及接口 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `m22:06:view` | 查看屈光分析 | 查看全部只读页面 | API-01~12、API-18、API-20~27、API-30 |
| `m22:06:create` | 新建投放台账 | 新建与提交台账 | API-13、API-14 |
| `m22:06:approve` | 审批投放台账 | 审批通过或驳回 | API-15、API-16 |
| `m22:06:edit` | 编辑投放台账 | 作废已审批台账 | API-17 |
| `m22:06:exec` | 执行重算 | 重算预测、生成报告 | API-28、API-06 |
| `m22:06:export` | 导出报表 | 生成与下载导出任务 | API-29、API-30 |
| `m22:06:admin` | 参数管理 | 价格带边界、告警阈值参数维护 | 配置类接口 |
| `m22:06:sensitive` | 敏感数据 | 查看医师个人维度业绩明细 | API-10、API-11、API-12、API-24 |

### 9.2 权限矩阵

| 角色 | P01 看板 | P02 漏斗 | P03 术式 | P04 医师 | P05 ROI | P06 质量 | P07 趋势 | P08 导出 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 院长 | ● | ● | ● | ● | △ | ● | ● | ● |
| 运营总监 | ● | ● | ● | ● | ● | ● | ● | ● |
| 屈光科室主任 | ● | ○ | ● | ● | △ | ● | ● | ● |
| 市场部经理 | ● | ● | △ | — | ● | — | ● | ● |
| 财务经理 | △ | — | △ | — | ● | △ | △ | ● |
| 医务科 / 质控专员 | △ | — | △ | ● | — | ● | △ | ● |
| 屈光咨询主管 | △ | ● | △ | — | — | — | △ | ○ |
| 物价管理员 | — | — | ● | — | — | — | — | ○ |

> 符号说明：`●` 完全控制、`○` 可操作、`◐` 仅本人数据、`△` 只读、`—` 无权限。

### 9.3 数据权限

| 编号 | 规则 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| DP-01 | 全院级用户（院长、运营总监）可见 `dept_id = 0` 全院数据与全部科室数据 | 由 `M22_SCOPE_HOSPITAL` 配置 |
| DP-02 | 科室级用户只能看到本科室的 `dept_id` 数据 | 请求中 `dept_id` 越权时自动降为本人科室 |
| DP-03 | 医师个人维度的敏感性数据（`m22:06:sensitive`）只有科室主任及以上可看 | 普通用户看医师行时姓名脱敏为「张**」 |
| DP-04 | 投放台账的新建权限归市场部，审批权限归财务经理，两者不得由同一人同时持有 | 通过角色互斥校验 |
| DP-05 | 内部契约 P-02 不走用户登录态，只校验服务间签名与 `X-Caller-Module` | 未登记调用方直接 403 |

### 9.4 安全措施

| 编号 | 措施 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| SC-01 | 内部接口鉴权 | `/internal/v1/m22/*` 走服务间签名（HMAC-SHA256 + 时间戳 ±300s） |
| SC-02 | 导出水印 | 每份导出文件写入「内部资料 + 单位 + 子模块 + 时间 + 导出人」 |
| SC-03 | 导出审计 | 谁、何时、什么模板、什么范围、多少行，全部落 `m22_refractive_log` |
| SC-04 | 患者隐私隔离 | 本模块统计粒度最小为「科室 × 渠道/术式/医师」，**不含患者维度**，任何接口不返回患者标识 |
| SC-05 | 台账不可篡改 | `APPROVED` 台账禁止 UPDATE，只能通过 `VOIDED` 作废并留痕 |
| SC-06 | 日志防删改 | `fn_m22_refractive_log_immutable()` 触发器阻断 UPDATE/DELETE |
| SC-07 | 预测明示 | 任何返回预测值的接口必须同时返回 `forecast_method` 与 `confidence_interval` |
| SC-08 | 幂等防重放 | 写接口必须带 `Idempotency-Key`，键值长度 8~64 |
